#### IMPORTERS.B_EMAIL · WHEN-VALIDATE-ITEM

- Level: Item · Owner: `IMPORTERS.B_EMAIL` · Execution hierarchy: default (Override)
- Lines: 29 total / 20 active (non-blank, non-comment)
- Built-ins: RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE
- Program units (form): –
- Library / DB package calls: STR.DISPALERT

```plsql
/* CGAP$OLES_SEQUENCE_BEFORE */
DECLARE
	EMAIL VARCHAR2(200);
	At_Pos number;
	Dot_Pos number;
	EMAIL_LENGTH  number;
	RET NUMBER;

Begin

	IF :System.TRIGGER_ITEM = :System.CURSOR_ITEM THEN

		EMAIL := :System.Cursor_Value;
		EMAIL_LENGTH := LENGTH(EMAIL);
		IF (EMAIL_LENGTH > 0)	 THEN
			At_Pos := INSTR(EMAIL, '@');
			Dot_Pos := INSTR(EMAIL, '.');

			--IF (At_Pos  <= 0 OR Dot_Pos <= 0 or At_Pos = 1 or Dot_Pos = EMAIL_LENGTH or Dot_Pos  < At_Pos or Dot_Pos  = At_Pos+1  ) THEN
				IF (At_Pos  <= 0 OR At_Pos = 1 ) THEN

				RET := STR.DISPALERT('خطأ فى صيغة البريد الالكتروني','INFORMATION');
				RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
			END IF;

		END IF;

	END IF;
End;
```

#### IMPORTERS.B_MOBILE_NO · WHEN-VALIDATE-ITEM

- Level: Item · Owner: `IMPORTERS.B_MOBILE_NO` · Execution hierarchy: default (Override) · Fire in query: false
- Lines: 34 total / 28 active (non-blank, non-comment)
- Built-ins: RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE
- Program units (form): –
- Library / DB package calls: STR.DISPALERT

```plsql
DECLARE
MOBILE_NO_length  NUMBER;
V    VARCHAR2(1);
BEGIN
   IF :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD = 99  THEN
       IF :GLOBAL.OLD_PORT_CD <> 99 AND :IMPR_NBR IS NOT NULL THEN
               STR.DISPALERT ('لا يمكن تعديل رقم الجوال, الرجاء استخدام الوظيفة الخاصة بتعديل رقم الجوال','ERROR');
				RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE ;
       END IF;
   END IF;
BEGIN
MOBILE_NO_length := LENGTH (:B_MOBILE_NO);

		FOR I IN  1 .. MOBILE_NO_length LOOP
		  V := SUBSTR(:B_MOBILE_NO,I,1);

		     IF ( V BETWEEN '0' AND '9' )
		     	 THEN
				NULL;
		     ELSE

				STR.DISPALERT ('يجب أن يكون رقم الجوال المدخل أرقام','information');
				RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
                 END IF;
  	END LOOP;
END ;

	IF :B_MOBILE_NO IS NOT NULL AND (LENGTH(:B_MOBILE_NO) != 9 OR SUBSTR(:B_MOBILE_NO,1,1) != 5 ) THEN
		STR.DISPALERT('رقم الجوال المدخل غير صحيح .. الرجاء اعادة الادخال ','ERROR');
		RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
	END IF;
END;
```

#### IMPORTERS.BANK_IBAN · WHEN-VALIDATE-ITEM

- Level: Item · Owner: `IMPORTERS.BANK_IBAN` · Execution hierarchy: default (Override) · Fire in query: false
- Lines: 15 total / 12 active (non-blank, non-comment)
- Built-ins: RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE
- Program units (form): –
- Library / DB package calls: STR.DISPALERT

```plsql
BEGIN

	:BANK_IBAN := UPPER(:BANK_IBAN);

	IF :BANK_IBAN ='SA' THEN
	   	:BANK_IBAN :=NULL;
	ELSIF :BANK_IBAN IS NOT NULL AND SUBSTR(:BANK_IBAN ,1,2)!='SA' THEN
	   	:BANK_IBAN := 'SA'||:BANK_IBAN ;
	END IF;

	IF :BANK_IBAN IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(:BANK_IBAN ) != 24 THEN
		STR.DISPALERT(' رقم الآيبان المدخل غير صحيح..الرجاء إعادة الإدخال ','ERROR');
		RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
	END IF;
END;
```

### 7.1 Record groups

#### CGDV$CREATION_FLAG
- Type: Query · Attached to LOV: yes · Comment: CGDV.D.CREATION_FLAG.L.M
- Columns: RV_LOW_VALUE (2), RV_MEANING (38)

```sql
SELECT  substr(RV_LOW_VALUE,1,2)  RV_LOW_VALUE
      , substr(RV_MEANING,1,38)  RV_MEANING
FROM    CG_REF_CODES
WHERE   RV_DOMAIN = 'CREATION_FLAG'
ORDER BY  RV_LOW_VALUE
```


#### CGFK$IMPORTERS_ADD_CTRY_CD
- Type: Query · Attached to LOV: yes
- Columns: L_CT_CITY_STATUS Number(1), ADD_CTRY_CD Number(3), ADD_CRTY_ARBC_NAME (50)

```sql
SELECT ADD_CITY.CITY_STATUS  L_CT_CITY_STATUS  /* CG$FK */
      ,ADD_CITY.CTRY_CD  ADD_CTRY_CD
      ,L_CTRY.CRTY_ARBC_NAME  ADD_CRTY_ARBC_NAME
      ,ADD_CITY.CITY_CD  CITY_CODE
      ,ADD_CITY.CITY_ARB_NAME  CITY_NAME
FROM   CITIES ADD_CITY
      ,COUNTRIES L_CTRY
WHERE  ((
(:SYSTEM.MODE = 'ENTER-QUERY')
)
OR ((:SYSTEM.MODE = 'NORMAL')
AND    ADD_CITY.CITY_STATUS = 1
))
AND    L_CTRY.CTRY_CD = ADD_CITY.CTRY_CD
```


#### CGFK$IMPORTERS_CITY_CODE_OF_ID
- Type: Query · Attached to LOV: yes
- Columns: L_CT_CITY_STATUS2 Number(1), CITY_CODE_OF_ID Number(3), ID_CITY_NAME (20), ID_MINS_COMM_CD Number(4)

```sql
SELECT ID_CITY.CITY_STATUS  L_CT_CITY_STATUS2  /* CG$FK */
      ,ID_CITY.CITY_CD  CITY_CODE_OF_ID
      ,ID_CITY.CITY_ARB_NAME  ID_CITY_NAME
      ,ID_CITY.MINS_COMM_CD  ID_MINS_COMM_CD
      ,ID_CITY.CTRY_CD  CTRY_CD_OF_ID
FROM   CITIES ID_CITY
WHERE  ((
(:SYSTEM.MODE = 'ENTER-QUERY')
)
OR ((:SYSTEM.MODE = 'NORMAL')
AND    ID_CITY.CITY_STATUS = 1
))
```


#### CGFK$IMPORTERS_CTRY_CD
- Type: Query · Attached to LOV: yes
- Columns: CTRY_CD Number(3), CRTY_ARBC_NAME (50), CTRY_CTRY_STATUS Number(1)

```sql
SELECT CTRY.CTRY_CD  CTRY_CD  /* CG$FK */
      ,CTRY.CRTY_ARBC_NAME  CRTY_ARBC_NAME
      ,CTRY.CTRY_STATUS  CTRY_CTRY_STATUS
FROM   COUNTRIES CTRY
WHERE  ((
(:SYSTEM.MODE = 'ENTER-QUERY')
)
OR ((:SYSTEM.MODE = 'NORMAL')
AND    CTRY.CTRY_STATUS = 1
))
```


#### CGFK$IMPORTERS_HQ_IMPR_NBR
- Type: Query · Attached to LOV: yes
- Columns: HQ_IMPR_NBR Number(10)

```sql
SELECT HQ_IMPR_NBR  HQ_IMPR_NBR  /* CG$FK */
FROM   HQ_IMPRS
```


#### CGFK$IMPORTERS_IMPR_TYPE_CD
- Type: Query · Attached to LOV: yes
- Columns: IMPR_TYPE_CD Number(3)

```sql
SELECT ITIDT.IMPR_TYPE_CD  IMPR_TYPE_CD  /* CG$FK */
      ,ITIDT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD  BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
      ,IT.IMPR_TYPE_NAME  IMPR_TYPE_NAME
      ,BIT.BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME  BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME
      ,ITIDT.NOTE  ITIDT_NOTE
      ,ITIDT.STATUS  STATUS
FROM   IMPR_TYPE_ID_TYPES ITIDT
      ,IMPR_TYPES IT
      ,BUS_ID_TYPES BIT
WHERE  IT.IMPR_TYPE_CD = ITIDT.IMPR_TYPE_CD
AND    BIT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = ITIDT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
```


#### CGFK$IMPORTERS_PORT_CD
- Type: Query · Attached to LOV: yes
- Columns: SDP_PORT_STATUS Number(1), PORT_CD Number(3), PORT_NAME (40)

```sql
SELECT L_SDP.PORT_STATUS  SDP_PORT_STATUS  /* CG$FK */
      ,L_SDP.PORT_CD  PORT_CD
      ,L_SDP.PORT_NAME  PORT_NAME
FROM   SD_PORTS L_SDP
```


#### CGDV$IMPORTERS_CUSTOMS_ENTERED
- Type: Static · Attached to LOV: yes · Comment: CGDV.C.IMPORTERS.CUSTOMS_ENTERED
- Columns: RV_LOW_VALUE (1), RV_MEANING (80)
- Static values: {"Index": "1", "Value": "0"}; {"Index": "2", "Value": "1"}; {"Index": "1", "Value": "السجل تم تسجيله بالشكل الطبيعي سواء مستورد سجل تجاري أو غيره"}; {"Index": "2", "Value": "بيانات المستورد تخص سجل تجاري غير موجود في جدول وزارة التجارة ، وتم تسجيله يدويا"}

```plsql
	PROCEDURE MOUSE_CLICK IS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	BEGIN
		Set_Item_Property(NAME_IN('SYSTEM.TRIGGER_ITEM'), VISUAL_ATTRIBUTE, 'STD_NORMAL_FUNCTION');
		Execute_Function(NAME_IN('SYSTEM.TRIGGER_ITEM'));
		GO_ITEM(NAME_IN('PARAMETER.P_CURRENT_ITEM'));
		COPY ('', 'PARAMETER.P_CURRENT_ITEM');

	END MOUSE_CLICK ;
```

### PU EXECUTE_FUNCTION (Procedure, 160 lines)

```plsql
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	PROCEDURE Execute_Function(FN In VARCHAR2) IS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	FOUND_CD BOOLEAN := FALSE;
	ROW_COUNT NUMBER := 1;
	CD_TITLE VARCHAR2(200);
	RET NUMBER;
	V_MSG VARCHAR2(2000);

	V_MOD_ID NUMBER;
	V_PATH VARCHAR2(200);
	pl_id   ParamList;
	V_CALLED_FORM VARCHAR2(200);
	V_MOD_NAME VARCHAR2(200);
	V_SEQ_NO NUMBER;
	V_SYS_TYPE	NUMBER;
	V_FILE_NAME VARCHAR2(200);

	cursor c_modudle_parameter(p_mid 	NUMBER)
	is select VAR_NAME ,VAR_VALUE
	from sc_port_setting
	where PORT_CD = :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD -- NALSALEH CENTRALIZED PROJECT
	AND sysobj_id = p_mid;

	BEGIN
	  IF FN IS NOT NULL THEN
			SET_VIEW_PROPERTY('LSIDE', VISIBLE, PROPERTY_FALSE);
			CD_TITLE := NAME_IN(FN);

	  	WHILE ROW_COUNT <= TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS_COUNT AND NOT FOUND_CD LOOP
	  		IF TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).TITLE LIKE CD_TITLE AND TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).ACT = 1 THEN
	  			FOUND_CD := TRUE;
	  		ELSE
		  		ROW_COUNT := ROW_COUNT + 1;
	  		END IF;

	  	END LOOP;

	  	IF FOUND_CD THEN
					IF TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).MID IS NOT NULL THEN
							BEGIN
						  	SELECT SYS_OS_FILE_NAME, SYS_ANAME, SYS_TYPE, SEQ_NO
					  		INTO V_FILE_NAME, V_MOD_NAME, V_SYS_TYPE, V_SEQ_NO
					  		FROM sc_system_objects
					  		WHERE SYS_TYPE IN (5,6)
					  		AND SYS_STATUS = 1
					  		AND SYSOBJ_ID = TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).MID;
							EXCEPTION
									WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
					  			STR.DISPALERT('الشاشة المطلوبة غير معرفة داخل نظام الأمن او انها غير فعالة' ,'ERROR');
							END;

							IF V_FILE_NAME IS NOT NULL THEN
						   pl_id := Get_Parameter_List('TMPLT_MOD_CALL');
						   IF Id_Null(pl_id) THEN
						     pl_id := Create_Parameter_List('TMPLT_MOD_CALL');
						     IF Id_Null(pl_id) THEN
						       ret := Str.Dispalert('Error creating parameter list TMPLT_MOD_CALL IN TEMPLATE ', 'SYSTEM_ERROR');
						       RAISE Form_Trigger_Failure;
						     END IF;

					      ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,'P_USER_ID',TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_USER_ID);
					      ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,'P_USER_NAME',TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_USER_NAME);
					      ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,'P_WS_ID',TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_WS_ID);
					      ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,'P_CURR_PORT_CD',TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD);
					      ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,'P_PORT_TYPE',TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_PORT_TYPE);
							  Add_Parameter(pl_id ,'P_GROUP_CD', TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_GROUP_CD);
							  Add_Parameter(pl_id ,'P_USER_TYPE', TEXT_PARAMETER, :PARAMETER.P_USER_TYPE);
							  Add_Parameter(pl_id ,'P_JOB_ID', TEXT_PARAMETER, :PARAMETER.P_JOB_ID);
							  Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'P_CLASS_CD', TEXT_PARAMETER, :PARAMETER.P_CLASS_CD);
					      ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,'P_MODULE_ID',TEXT_PARAMETER,TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).MID);
					      ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,'P_MODULE_NAME',TEXT_PARAMETER,V_MOD_NAME);
						    ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_BRKR_PORT_CD' ,TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_BRKR_PORT_CD);
		     				ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_SHPG_AGNT_NBR',TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_SHPG_AGNT_NBR);
		     				ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_BRKR_NBR'      ,TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_BRKR_NBR);
		     				ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_LIC_TYPE'     ,TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_LIC_TYPE);
		     				ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_BANK_CD'      ,TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_BANK_CD);
		     				ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_BANK_BRNCH_CD',TEXT_PARAMETER,:PARAMETER.P_BANK_BRNCH_CD);
						    for i in c_modudle_parameter(TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).MID)
				  			loop
				  				ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id ,i.VAR_NAME,TEXT_PARAMETER,i.VAR_VALUE);
				  			end loop;

								EXEC_FUNS(TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).CD);
						    pl_id := Get_Parameter_List('TMPLT_MOD_CALL');
					   		IF NOT Id_Null(pl_id) THEN
								  	V_CALLED_FORM := TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).PATH;
								  	IF SUBSTR(V_CALLED_FORM, LENGTH(V_CALLED_FORM), 1) <> '\' THEN
								  		V_CALLED_FORM := V_CALLED_FORM||'\';
								  	END IF;


								  	V_CALLED_FORM := V_CALLED_FORM||V_FILE_NAME;
										IF V_SYS_TYPE = 5 THEN
											  CALL_FORM(V_CALLED_FORM,
										              no_hide,
										              no_replace,
										              no_query_only,
										              pl_id);
										ELSE
												DEFAULT_VALUE('0', 'GLOBAL.SYSTEM_OBJ');
						     				ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_SYS_ID',TEXT_PARAMETER,:GLOBAL.SYSTEM_OBJ);
	  	   								ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_REP_NO',TEXT_PARAMETER,V_SEQ_NO);
                        ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'P_REPORT_NO',TEXT_PARAMETER,:GLOBAL.SYSTEM_OBJ ||'-'||V_SEQ_NO);
									     IF TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).FORM_FLAG = 1 THEN
												  CALL_FORM(V_CALLED_FORM,
											              no_hide,
											              no_replace,
											              no_query_only,
											              pl_id);
									     ELSIF TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).PRNT_FLAG = 1 THEN
	      									ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'DESTYPE',TEXT_PARAMETER,'PRINTER');
	      									Add_Parameter(pl_id,'PARAMFORM',TEXT_PARAMETER,'NO');
	      									ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'ORACLE_SHUTDOWN',TEXT_PARAMETER,'YES');

	      									IF ADJ_PRN.ADJ_REPORT_PRINTER(TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).MID, V_MSG) = TRUE THEN
									  				REP.Run_Product(REPORTS,V_CALLED_FORM,SYNCHRONOUS,BATCH,FILESYSTEM,pl_id,NULL);
	      									ELSIF V_MSG IS NOT NULL THEN
	      										STR.DISPALERT(V_MSG, 'ERROR');
	      									ELSE
	      										STR.DISPALERT('لا يمكن ضبط الطابعة للتقرير', 'ERROR');
	      									END IF;
									     ELSE
	      									Add_Parameter(pl_id,'PARAMFORM',TEXT_PARAMETER,'NO');
	      									ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'ORACLE_SHUTDOWN',TEXT_PARAMETER,'YES');
	      									ADD_PARAMETER(pl_id,'MAXIMIZE',TEXT_PARAMETER,'YES');
	      									IF ADJ_PRN.ADJ_REPORT_PRINTER(TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).MID, V_MSG) = TRUE THEN
										  			REP.Run_Product(REPORTS,V_CALLED_FORM,SYNCHRONOUS,RUNTIME,FILESYSTEM,pl_id,NULL);
	      									ELSIF V_MSG IS NOT NULL THEN
	      										STR.DISPALERT(V_MSG, 'ERROR');
	      									ELSE
	      										STR.DISPALERT('لا يمكن ضبط الطابعة للتقرير', 'ERROR');
	      									END IF;

	     								 END IF;
										END IF;

										:PARAMETER.P_CTRL_LAST_EXEC_CD := TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).CD;
									  EXEC_FUNS('MOD_CALL_BACK');
									  :PARAMETER.P_CTRL_LAST_EXEC_CD := NULL;
	  						    Destroy_Parameter_List(pl_id);
					   		END IF;
							 ELSE
							   ret := Str.DispAlert('Parameter list '||'TMPLT_MOD_CALL'||' already exists!','INFORMATION');
							   RAISE Form_Trigger_Failure;
							 END IF;
						  ELSE
				  			RET := STR.DISPALERT('الشاشة المطلوبة غير معرفة داخل نظام الأمن او انها غير فعالة' ,'ERROR');
						  END IF;
				ELSE
						EXEC_FUNS(TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).CD);
						:PARAMETER.P_CTRL_LAST_EXEC_CD := TMPLT.FUN_ITEMS(ROW_COUNT).CD;
					  EXEC_FUNS('MOD_CALL_BACK');
					  :PARAMETER.P_CTRL_LAST_EXEC_CD := NULL;
				END IF;
	  	ELSE
	  		RET := STR.DISPALERT('خطأ داخلي في أكواد الوظائف - إتصل بالمساندة الفنية' ,'ERROR');
	  	END IF;
	  END IF;
	END Execute_Function;
```

### PU CGDV$POPULATE_DYNAMIC_LISTS (Procedure, 25 lines)

```plsql
/* CGDV$POPULATE_DYNAMIC_LISTS */
PROCEDURE CGDV$POPULATE_DYNAMIC_LISTS IS
/* Read each lists values from its record group and populate the list */
  temp NUMBER;  /* Return value of populate_group call */
  rg_id RECORDGROUP;  /* Return value of populate_group call */
BEGIN
  rg_id := create_group_from_query('CGDV$DLRG',
    'SELECT RV_MEANING, RV_LOW_VALUE '||
    'FROM   CG_REF_CODES '||
    'WHERE  RV_DOMAIN = ''COMM_RGSTRTN_TYPE'' '||
    'ORDER BY  2');
  temp := populate_group(rg_id);
  populate_list('IMPORTERS.COMM_RGSTRTN_TYPE', rg_id);
  delete_group(rg_id);

  rg_id := create_group_from_query('CGDV$DLRG',
    'SELECT RV_MEANING, RV_LOW_VALUE '||
    'FROM   CG_REF_CODES '||
    'WHERE  RV_DOMAIN = ''ALLOWED EXPORT'' '||
    'ORDER BY  2');
  temp := populate_group(rg_id);
  populate_list('IMPORTERS.IMPR_EXPR_TYPE', rg_id);
  delete_group(rg_id);

END;
```

### PU CGFK$QRY_IMPORTERS_IMP_SDP_FK (Procedure, 25 lines)

```plsql
/* CGFK$QRY_IMPORTERS_IMP_SDP_FK */
PROCEDURE CGFK$QRY_IMPORTERS_IMP_SDP_FK(
   P_PORT_NAME       IN OUT VARCHAR2      /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.PORT_NAME.       */
  ,P_SDP_PORT_STATUS IN OUT NUMBER        /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.SDP_PORT_STATUS. */
  ,P_PORT_CD         IN     NUMBER  ) IS  /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.PORT_CD.         */
/* Query foreign key value/query lookup data. */
BEGIN
  DECLARE
    CURSOR C IS
      SELECT L_SDP.PORT_STATUS
            ,L_SDP.PORT_NAME
      FROM   SD_PORTS L_SDP
      WHERE  L_SDP.PORT_CD = P_PORT_CD;
  BEGIN
    OPEN C;
    FETCH C
    INTO    P_SDP_PORT_STATUS
          , P_PORT_NAME;
    IF C%NOTFOUND THEN
      CLOSE C;
      RAISE NO_DATA_FOUND;
    END IF;
    CLOSE C;
  END;
END;
```

```plsql
/* CGFK$QRY_IMPORTERS_IMP_ITIDT_F */
PROCEDURE CGFK$QRY_IMPORTERS_IMP_ITIDT_F(
   P_BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME IN OUT VARCHAR2      /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME. */
  ,P_IMPR_TYPE_NAME   IN OUT VARCHAR2      /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TYPE_NAME.   */
  ,P_STATUS           IN OUT NUMBER        /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.STATUS.           */
  ,P_ITIDT_NOTE       IN OUT VARCHAR2      /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.ITIDT_NOTE.       */
  ,P_IMPR_TYPE_CD     IN     NUMBER        /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TYPE_CD.     */
  ,P_BUS_ID_TYPE_CD   IN     NUMBER  ) IS  /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD.   */
/* Query foreign key value/query lookup data. */
BEGIN
  DECLARE
    CURSOR C IS
      SELECT ITIDT.NOTE
            ,ITIDT.STATUS
            ,IT.IMPR_TYPE_NAME
            ,BIT.BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME
      FROM   IMPR_TYPE_ID_TYPES ITIDT
            ,IMPR_TYPES IT
            ,BUS_ID_TYPES BIT
      WHERE  ITIDT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = P_BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
      AND    ITIDT.IMPR_TYPE_CD = P_IMPR_TYPE_CD
      AND    IT.IMPR_TYPE_CD = ITIDT.IMPR_TYPE_CD
      AND    BIT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = ITIDT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD;
  BEGIN
    OPEN C;
    FETCH C
    INTO    P_ITIDT_NOTE
          , P_STATUS
          , P_IMPR_TYPE_NAME
          , P_BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME;
    IF C%NOTFOUND THEN
      CLOSE C;
      RAISE NO_DATA_FOUND;
    END IF;
    CLOSE C;
  END;
END;
```

```plsql
/* CGFK$CHK_IMPORTERS_IMP_ITIDT_F */
PROCEDURE CGFK$CHK_IMPORTERS_IMP_ITIDT_F(
   P_BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME IN OUT VARCHAR2      /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME. */
  ,P_IMPR_TYPE_NAME   IN OUT VARCHAR2      /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TYPE_NAME.   */
  ,P_STATUS           IN OUT NUMBER        /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.STATUS.           */
  ,P_ITIDT_NOTE       IN OUT VARCHAR2      /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.ITIDT_NOTE.       */
  ,P_IMPR_TYPE_CD     IN     NUMBER        /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TYPE_CD.     */
  ,P_BUS_ID_TYPE_CD   IN     NUMBER  ) IS  /* Value in Item :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD.   */
/* Validate foreign key value/query lookup data. */
BEGIN
  DECLARE
    CURSOR C IS
      SELECT ITIDT.NOTE
            ,ITIDT.STATUS
            ,IT.IMPR_TYPE_NAME
            ,BIT.BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME
      FROM   IMPR_TYPE_ID_TYPES ITIDT
            ,IMPR_TYPES IT
            ,BUS_ID_TYPES BIT
      WHERE  ITIDT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = P_BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
      AND    ITIDT.IMPR_TYPE_CD = P_IMPR_TYPE_CD
      AND    IT.IMPR_TYPE_CD = ITIDT.IMPR_TYPE_CD
      AND    BIT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = ITIDT.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD;
  BEGIN
    OPEN C;
```

```plsql
/* CGSF$CLRLU_IMPORTERS_IMP_BUS_I */
PROCEDURE CGSF$CLRLU_IMPORTERS_IMP_BUS_I IS
/* This clears all items associated with the given foreign key. */
BEGIN
  IF (:IMPORTERS.CITY_CODE_OF_ID IS NOT NULL) THEN
    :IMPORTERS.CITY_CODE_OF_ID := NULL;
  END IF;
  IF (:IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD_OF_ID IS NOT NULL) THEN
    :IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD_OF_ID := NULL;
  END IF;
  IF (:IMPORTERS.L_CT_CITY_STATUS2 IS NOT NULL) THEN
    :IMPORTERS.L_CT_CITY_STATUS2 := NULL;
  END IF;
  IF (:IMPORTERS.ID_CITY_NAME IS NOT NULL) THEN
    :IMPORTERS.ID_CITY_NAME := NULL;
  END IF;
  IF (:IMPORTERS.ID_MINS_COMM_CD IS NOT NULL) THEN
    :IMPORTERS.ID_MINS_COMM_CD := NULL;
  END IF;
END;
```

### PU CGTE$CHECK_CONSTRAINT_VIO (Function, 53 lines)

```plsql
/* CGTE$CHECK_CONSTRAINT_VIO */
FUNCTION CGTE$CHECK_CONSTRAINT_VIO(
   P_SERVER_ERR IN NUMBER        /* Server error message */
  ,P_SERVER_MSG IN VARCHAR2)     /* Server error number  */
RETURN BOOLEAN IS
/* Check and report Primary/Unique Key, Check Constraint and User */
/* Defined Constraint violations                                  */
  constraint_name VARCHAR2(61);  /* Violated constraint */
BEGIN
  /* Check if the error code is one we are interested in */
  IF ( NOT (P_SERVER_ERR IN  (1, 2290) OR
           (P_SERVER_ERR >= 20000 AND P_SERVER_ERR <= 20999) ) )
  THEN
    RETURN( FALSE );
  END IF;

  /* Deal with user defined errors */
  IF (P_SERVER_ERR >= 20000 AND P_SERVER_ERR <= 20999) THEN
    Des$Form_errors.PUSH(''||CGTE$STRIP_FIRST_ERROR(P_SERVER_MSG), 'E', 'OFG', 0);
    RETURN( TRUE );
  END IF;

  /* Deal with Primary/Unique Key and Check Constraint violations */
  constraint_name := CGTE$STRIP_CONSTRAINT(P_SERVER_MSG);
  IF (P_SERVER_ERR = 1) THEN
    IF (constraint_name = 'IMP_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH('هذا السجل مدخل سابقاً - أعد ادخال البيانات', 'E', 'OFG', 0);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'IMP_BUSID_UK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH('هذا السجل مدخل سابقاً - أعد ادخال البيانات', 'E', 'OFG', 0);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'SDP_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH(Des$Form_errors.MSGGETTEXT(6, 'Row exists already with same <p1>', 'رمز الجمرك' ), 'E', 'OFG', 6);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'ITIDT_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH('هذا السجل مدخل سابقاً - أعد ادخال البيانات', 'E', 'OFG', 0);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'IT_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH('هذا السجل مدخل سابقاً - أعد ادخال البيانات', 'E', 'OFG', 0);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'BIT_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH('هذا السجل مدخل سابقاً - أعد ادخال البيانات', 'E', 'OFG', 0);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'CT_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH(Des$Form_errors.MSGGETTEXT(6, 'Row exists already with same <p1>', 'City Cd,رمز الدولة' ), 'E', 'OFG', 6);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'CT_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH(Des$Form_errors.MSGGETTEXT(6, 'Row exists already with same <p1>', 'City Cd,رمز الدولة' ), 'E', 'OFG', 6);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'CTRY_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH(Des$Form_errors.MSGGETTEXT(6, 'Row exists already with same <p1>', 'رمز الدولة' ), 'E', 'OFG', 6);
    ELSIF (constraint_name = 'CTRY_PK') THEN
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH(Des$Form_errors.MSGGETTEXT(6, 'Row exists already with same <p1>', 'Ctry Cd' ), 'E', 'OFG', 6);
    ELSE
      Des$Form_errors.PUSH(P_SERVER_MSG, 'I', 'OFG', 0);
    END IF;
  ELSIF (P_SERVER_ERR = 2290) THEN
    Des$Form_errors.PUSH(P_SERVER_MSG, 'I', 'OFG', 0);
  END IF;
  RETURN( TRUE );
END;
```

```plsql
/* Formatted on 14/01/20 10:19:42 ? (QP5 v5.294) */
PROCEDURE update_importer
IS
   ret         NUMBER;
   v_chk_upd   NUMBER;
   v_msg       VARCHAR2 (500);
   msg_lvl     VARCHAR2 (5);
   MSG_CD      NUMBER;
BEGIN
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   BEGIN
      msg_lvl := :SYSTEM.message_level;
      :SYSTEM.message_level := '25';
      FORMS_DDL ('COMMIT');
      :SYSTEM.message_level := msg_lvl;
   EXCEPTION
      WHEN OTHERS
      THEN
         NULL;
   END;

   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   IF :importers.port_cd IS NOT NULL AND :importers.impr_nbr IS NOT NULL
   THEN
      IF :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = 2 THEN
      V_MSG :=
            'السجل الرئيسي =  '
         || :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
         || 'السجل الفرعي = '
         || :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_BRNCH
         || ' تأكيد التحديث؟';
    END IF ;
    IF :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TYPE_CD = 4 AND :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = 5 THEN
      V_MSG :=
            'رقم مدني =  '
         || :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
         || ' تأكيد التحديث؟';
    END IF ;
     IF :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TYPE_CD = 4 AND :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = 3 THEN
      V_MSG :=
            ' رقم الاقامة =  '
         || :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
         || ' تأكيد التحديث؟';
     END IF ;

      ret := str.dispalert (v_msg, 'CONFIRM');

      IF ret = alert_button1
      THEN
         msg_lvl := :SYSTEM.message_level;
         :SYSTEM.message_level := '25';

         BEGIN
            LIB_USR.impr_utilities.UPDATE_IMPORTERS_NAMES (
               :importers.port_cd,
               :importers.impr_nbr,
               MSG_CD,
               v_msg);
            ret := str.dispalert (v_msg, 'INFORMATION');
         EXCEPTION
            WHEN OTHERS
            THEN
               NULL;
         END;
      END IF;
   END IF;
END;
```

### PU UPDATE_BASIC_IMPORTER (Procedure, 24 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE UPDATE_BASIC_IMPORTER IS
X NUMBER;
BEGIN
SELECT COUNT(*) INTO X FROM IMPORTER_BASIC_DATA
WHERE  BUS_ID=:BUS_ID AND BUS_ID_BRNCH = NVL(:BUS_ID_BRNCH,0)
AND IMPR_TYPE_CD=:IMPR_TYPE_CD AND BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = :BUS_ID_TYPE_CD;
IF X = 0 THEN
INSERT INTO IMPORTER_BASIC_DATA
   (BUS_ID, BUS_ID_BRNCH,
    IMPR_TYPE_CD, BUS_ID_TYPE_CD, MOBILE_NO, EMAIL,ID_NBR,HQ_IMPR_NBR)
 VALUES
  (:BUS_ID, NVL(:BUS_ID_BRNCH,0),
    :IMPR_TYPE_CD, :BUS_ID_TYPE_CD, :B_MOBILE_NO,:B_EMAIL,:B_ID_NBR,:HQ_IMPR_NBR);
ELSE
UPDATE IMPORTER_BASIC_DATA SET MOBILE_NO=NVL(:IMPORTERS.B_MOBILE_NO,MOBILE_NO),
    EMAIL=NVL(:IMPORTERS.B_EMAIL,EMAIL),
    ID_NBR=NVL(:IMPORTERS.B_ID_NBR,ID_NBR),
    HQ_IMPR_NBR=NVL(:HQ_IMPR_NBR,HQ_IMPR_NBR)
WHERE  BUS_ID=:BUS_ID AND NVL(BUS_ID_BRNCH,0) = NVL(:BUS_ID_BRNCH,0)
AND IMPR_TYPE_CD=:IMPR_TYPE_CD AND BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = :BUS_ID_TYPE_CD;

END IF;
STANDARD.COMMIT;
END;
```

```plsql
PROCEDURE LOV_UPDATE_MOBLIE IS
V_CNT_RASEL      NUMBER;
V_CNT_INFO_RASEL NUMBER;
v_filler1      VARCHAR2(200);
 pl_id   ParamList;
   pl_name VARCHAR2(100) := 'LOV_CONFIRM_TAR';
   RET NUMBER;
   V_RESULT NUMBER;
   V_CENTRAL_MOBILE NUMBER;
  P_RESULT_CD NUMBER;
  P_MSG  VARCHAR2(50);
BEGIN

	   pl_id := Get_Parameter_List(pl_name);
  IF Id_Null(pl_id) THEN
     pl_id := Create_Parameter_List(pl_name);
     IF Id_Null(pl_id) THEN
       ret := Str.Dispalert('Error creating parameter list '||pl_name, 'SYSTEM_ERROR');
       RAISE Form_Trigger_Failure;
     END IF;

      BEGIN
     	 SELECT MOBILE_NO
     	   INTO V_CENTRAL_MOBILE
     	    FROM IMPORTERS_CENTRAL
     	     WHERE PORT_CD    = :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD AND IMPR_NBR = :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR ;

      EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
      	 V_CENTRAL_MOBILE := NULL;

     	END;
   Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'WINDOW_WIDTH', TEXT_PARAMETER, 5);
  -- Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'ROW_COUNT', TEXT_PARAMETER, 10);
   Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'WINDOW_TITLE', TEXT_PARAMETER, 'تعديل رقم الجوال');
   Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'P_CENTRAL_MOBILE', TEXT_PARAMETER, V_CENTRAL_MOBILE);


          CALL_FORM('LOV_UPDATE_IMPR_MOBILE.fmx',
                  no_hide,
              no_replace,
              no_query_only,
              pl_id);
          		Destroy_Parameter_List(pl_id);
	ELSE
		ret := Str.DispAlert('Parameter list '||pl_name||' already EXISTS!','INFORMATION');
		RAISE Form_Trigger_Failure;
	END IF;


   IF    :GLOBAL.MOBILE_NO  IS NOT NULL THEN

	 		impr.CHK_MOBILE_FORMAT (:GLOBAL.MOBILE_NO  , V_RESULT );
	        IF V_RESULT = 0 THEN
		 	      STR.DISPALERT('رقم الجوال المدخل غير صحيح' ,'ERROR');
		       RAISE Form_Trigger_Failure;

           END IF ;
     IMPR_UTILITIES.UPDATE_IMPR_MOBILE_NO (:IMPR_NBR , :GLOBAL.MOBILE_NO ,P_RESULT_CD , P_MSG ) ;

        IF P_RESULT_CD = 1 THEN
       /* UPDATE  IMPORTERS_CENTRAL
         SET    MOBILE_NO         = :GLOBAL.MOBILE_NO
         WHERE  IMPR_NBR          = :IMPR_NBR ;*/
   	   	   STANDARD.COMMIT;
		STR.DISPALERT('تمت العملية بنجاح','INFORMATION');
        END IF;
  END IF;
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


END;
```

### PU IS_DIGIT (Function, 17 lines)

```plsql
FUNCTION IS_Digit(txt_num VARCHAR2) RETURN BOOLEAN IS
 chr1      VARCHAR2(200);
 len_txt   NUMBER ;
 counter   NUMBER :=1;

BEGIN
 len_txt:=LENGTH(txt_num);
 WHILE (counter<=len_txt) LOOP
    chr1 := SUBSTR(txt_num,counter,1) ;
    IF ASCII(chr1) <48  OR ASCII(chr1)>57 THEN
       RETURN FALSE;
     END IF;
     counter :=counter+1;
 END LOOP;
     RETURN TRUE;
END;
```

```plsql
	Ret NUMBER;
	V_ACT_DT DATE;
 	V_USER_ID  VARCHAR2(11);
 	v_impr_master_nbr   IMPORTERS.IMPR_MASTER_NBR%TYPE;
      v_act_status        IMPORTERS.ACT_STATUS%TYPE;
BEGIN
IF (:IMPORTERS.Impr_Nbr IS NOT NULL) THEN
SELECT SYSDATE INTO V_ACT_DT FROM dual;
V_USER_ID := :PARAMETER.P_USER_ID;



               BEGIN
                  SELECT impr_master_nbr, act_status
                    INTO v_impr_master_nbr, v_act_status
                    FROM IMPORTERS
                   WHERE   PORT_CD    = :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD
				AND   bus_id_type_cd = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
                         AND bus_id = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
                         AND bus_id_brnch = :IMPORTERS.bus_id_brnch
                         AND impr_type_cd = :IMPORTERS.impr_type_cd
                         AND ctry_cd = :IMPORTERS.ctry_cd
                         AND ROWNUM = 1;
               EXCEPTION
                  WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND
                  THEN
                     v_impr_master_nbr := NULL;
                     v_act_status := NULL;
               END;




-- ADD COLUMN(FILLER1)INTO INSERT QUERY --
INSERT INTO IMPR_UPDTS(Port_Cd, IMPR_NBR, HQ_IMPR_NBR, IMPR_TYPE_CD, BUS_ID_TYPE_CD, BUS_ID , BUS_ID_BRNCH, BUS_ID_EXPRD_DT, CTRY_CD_OF_ID, CITY_CODE_OF_ID, IMPR_EXPR_TYPE, ARBC_IMPR_NAME, ENG_IMPR_NAME, IMPR_NAME_STD, IMPR_NBR_PARENT,
			     IMPR_CNTCT_NAME, IMPR_TEL_NBR, IMPR_POB_NBR, IMPR_ZIP_CD, IMPR_FAX_NBR, IMPR_TELEX_NBR,  EMAIL, CTRY_CD, ADD_CTRY_CD, CITY_CODE, LST_TRAN_DT, IMPD_STMT_NBR, IMPR_DATA_SRCE, ACT_DT, ACT_TYPE, USR_ID,COMM_RGSTRTN_TYPE,CAR_AGENT_FLAG,MOBILE_NO,BANK_IBAN, EXPRESS_POST
-- --> 23/08/2014 AHMED A-MONIM  consume service from Yesser Producion
			     ,ID_NBR
                       ,BUS_ID_ISSUE_DT,BUS_ID_STATUS,CREATION_FLAG
                       ,IMPR_ADDRESS ,impr_master_nbr ,act_status
                                      )
	 VALUES(:IMPORTERS.Port_Cd, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR, :IMPORTERS.HQ_IMPR_NBR, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TYPE_CD, :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD,:IMPORTERS.BUS_ID , :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_BRNCH, :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_EXPRD_DT, :IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD_OF_ID, :IMPORTERS.CITY_CODE_OF_ID, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_EXPR_TYPE,:IMPORTERS.ARBC_IMPR_NAME, :IMPORTERS.ENG_IMPR_NAME, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NAME_STD, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR_PARENT,
		  :IMPORTERS.IMPR_CNTCT_NAME, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TEL_NBR, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_POB_NBR, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_ZIP_CD, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_FAX_NBR, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_TELEX_NBR, :IMPORTERS.EMAIL, :IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD, :IMPORTERS.ADD_CTRY_CD, :IMPORTERS.CITY_CODE, :IMPORTERS.LST_TRAN_DT, :IMPORTERS.IMPD_STMT_NBR, :IMPORTERS.IMPR_DATA_SRCE,  V_ACT_DT, V_ACT_TYPE, V_USER_ID,:IMPORTERS.COMM_RGSTRTN_TYPE,
		  :IMPORTERS.CAR_AGENT_FLAG,:IMPORTERS.MOBILE_NO,:IMPORTERS.BANK_IBAN, :IMPORTERS.EXPRESS_POST
-- --> 23/08/2014 AHMED A-MONIM  consume service from Yesser Producion
		  ,:IMPORTERS.ID_NBR
              ,:IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_ISSUE_DT,:IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_STATUS,:IMPORTERS.CREATION_FLAG
              ,:IMPORTERS.IMPR_ADDRESS , v_impr_master_nbr , v_act_status
                                        );

ELSE
	Ret := STR.DispArbMsg(9, 140,'ERROR');
	RAISE FORm_Trigger_Failure;
END IF;
END;
```

### PU GET_CR_DATA (Procedure, 401 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE get_cr_data (p_string_result VARCHAR2, p_type VARCHAR2)
IS
   FOUND               NUMBER;
   string_result       VARCHAR2 (5000);
   v_pos_1             NUMBER;
   v_pos_2             NUMBER;
   v_pos_3             NUMBER;
   v_pos_4             NUMBER;
   v_pos_5             NUMBER;
   v_pos_6             NUMBER;
   v_pos_7             NUMBER;
   v_pos_8             NUMBER;
   v_pos_9             NUMBER;
   v_pos_10            NUMBER;
   v_pos_11            NUMBER;
   v_pos_12            NUMBER;
   v_pos_13            NUMBER;
   v_pos_14            NUMBER;
   v_pos_15            NUMBER;
   v_pos_16            NUMBER;
   v_pos_17            NUMBER;
   v_pos_18            NUMBER;
   v_pos_19            NUMBER;
   v_pos_20            NUMBER;
   v_pos_21            NUMBER;
   v_txt_1             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_2             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_3             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_4             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_5             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_6             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_7             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_8             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_9             VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_10            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_11            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_12            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_13            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_14            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_15            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_16            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_17            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_18            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_19            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_20            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_txt_21            VARCHAR2 (500);
   v_bus_id_exprd_dt   importers.bus_id_exprd_dt%TYPE;
   v_bus_id_issue_dt   importers.bus_id_issue_dt%TYPE;
   v_arbc_impr_name    importers.arbc_impr_name%TYPE;
   v_impr_name_std     importers.impr_name_std%TYPE;
   v_impr_address      importers.impr_address%TYPE;
   v_impr_tel_nbr      importers.impr_tel_nbr%TYPE;
   v_impr_fax_nbr      importers.impr_fax_nbr%TYPE;
   v_impr_telex_nbr    importers.impr_telex_nbr%TYPE;
   v_activity          importers.activity%TYPE;
   v_bus_id_status     importers.bus_id_status%TYPE;
   v_city_code_of_id   importers.city_code_of_id%TYPE;
   v_port_cd           importers.port_cd%TYPE;
   v_impr_nbr          importers.impr_nbr%TYPE;
   v_hq_impr_nbr       importers.hq_impr_nbr%TYPE;
   v_bus_id            importers.bus_id%TYPE;
   v_bus_id_brnch      importers.bus_id_brnch%TYPE;
BEGIN
   str.dispalert
      ('سيتم الحصول على بيانات المستورد من وزارة التجارة.....الرجاء الإنتظار',
       'ERROR'
      );
   string_result := p_string_result;

   IF string_result IS NOT NULL
   THEN
      string_result :=
            LTRIM (RTRIM (string_result))
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |'
         || ' |';

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1)
        INTO v_pos_1
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_1)
        INTO v_pos_2
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_2)
        INTO v_pos_3
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_3)
        INTO v_pos_4
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_4)
        INTO v_pos_5
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_5)
        INTO v_pos_6
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_6)
        INTO v_pos_7
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_7)
        INTO v_pos_8
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_8)
        INTO v_pos_9
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_9)
        INTO v_pos_10
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_10)
        INTO v_pos_11
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_11)
        INTO v_pos_12
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_12)
        INTO v_pos_13
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_13)
        INTO v_pos_14
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_14)
        INTO v_pos_15
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_15)
        INTO v_pos_16
        FROM DUAL;

      SELECT INSTR (string_result, '|', 1 + v_pos_16)
        INTO v_pos_17
        FROM DUAL;

--            SELECT INSTR(STRING_RESULT,'|',1 + V_POS_17 ) INTO V_POS_18 FROM DUAL;
--            SELECT INSTR(STRING_RESULT,'|',1 + V_POS_18 ) INTO V_POS_19 FROM DUAL;
--            SELECT INSTR(STRING_RESULT,'|',1 + V_POS_19 ) INTO V_POS_20 FROM DUAL;
--            SELECT INSTR(STRING_RESULT,'|',1 + V_POS_20 ) INTO V_POS_21 FROM DUAL;
      v_txt_1 :=
              UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result, 1, v_pos_1 - (1)))));
      v_txt_2 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_1 + 1,
                                      v_pos_2 - (v_pos_1 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_3 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_2 + 1,
                                      v_pos_3 - (v_pos_2 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_4 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_3 + 1,
                                      v_pos_4 - (v_pos_3 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_5 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_4 + 1,
                                      v_pos_5 - (v_pos_4 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_6 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_5 + 1,
                                      v_pos_6 - (v_pos_5 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_7 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_6 + 1,
                                      v_pos_7 - (v_pos_6 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_8 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_7 + 1,
                                      v_pos_8 - (v_pos_7 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_9 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_8 + 1,
                                      v_pos_9 - (v_pos_8 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_10 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_9 + 1,
                                      v_pos_10 - (v_pos_9 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_11 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_10 + 1,
                                      v_pos_11 - (v_pos_10 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_12 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_11 + 1,
                                      v_pos_12 - (v_pos_11 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_13 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_12 + 1,
                                      v_pos_13 - (v_pos_12 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_14 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_13 + 1,
                                      v_pos_14 - (v_pos_13 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_15 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_14 + 1,
                                      v_pos_15 - (v_pos_14 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );
      v_txt_16 :=
         UPPER (LTRIM (RTRIM (SUBSTR (string_result,
                                      v_pos_15 + 1,
                                      v_pos_16 - (v_pos_15 + 1)
                                     )
                             )
                      )
               );

--            V_TXT_17 := UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(SUBSTR( STRING_RESULT ,  V_POS_16 + 1 , V_POS_17 - (V_POS_16 + 1 ) )))) ;
--            V_TXT_18 := UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(SUBSTR( STRING_RESULT ,  V_POS_17 + 1 , V_POS_18 - (V_POS_17 + 1 ) )))) ;
--            V_TXT_19 := UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(SUBSTR( STRING_RESULT ,  V_POS_18 + 1 , V_POS_19 - (V_POS_18 + 1 ) )))) ;
--            V_TXT_20 := UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(SUBSTR( STRING_RESULT ,  V_POS_19 + 1 , V_POS_20 - (V_POS_19 + 1 ) )))) ;
--            V_TXT_21 := UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(SUBSTR( STRING_RESULT ,  V_POS_20 + 1 , V_POS_21 - (V_POS_20 + 1 ) )))) ;
      IF v_txt_1 IS NOT NULL
      THEN
         v_bus_id_exprd_dt := TO_DATE (v_txt_3, 'MI-MM-YYYY');
         v_bus_id_issue_dt := TO_DATE (v_txt_4, 'MI-MM-YYYY');
         v_arbc_impr_name := v_txt_6;
         v_impr_address := v_txt_8;

         IF v_txt_9 > '0000000'
         THEN
            v_impr_tel_nbr := v_txt_9;
         ELSIF v_txt_10 > '0000000'
         THEN
            v_impr_tel_nbr := v_txt_10;
         END IF;

         IF v_txt_11 > '0000000'
         THEN
            v_impr_fax_nbr := v_txt_11;
         ELSIF v_txt_12 > '0000000'
         THEN
            v_impr_fax_nbr := v_txt_12;
         END IF;

         v_impr_telex_nbr := v_txt_13;
         v_activity := v_txt_14;

         IF UPPER (v_txt_15) = 'ACTIVE'
         THEN
            v_bus_id_status := 1;
         ELSIF UPPER (v_txt_15) = 'EXPIRED'
         THEN
            v_bus_id_status := 2;
         ELSIF UPPER (v_txt_15) = 'SUSPENDED'
         THEN
            v_bus_id_status := 3;
         ELSIF UPPER (v_txt_15) LIKE 'WRITTEN%OFF'
         THEN
            v_bus_id_status := 4;
         ELSIF UPPER (v_txt_15) LIKE 'CANCELLED'
         THEN
            v_bus_id_status := 4;
         ELSE
            v_bus_id_status := 5;
         END IF;

         BEGIN
            SELECT city_cd
              INTO v_city_code_of_id
              FROM cities
             WHERE mins_comm_cd = v_txt_16;
         EXCEPTION
            WHEN OTHERS
            THEN
               NULL;
         END;

         v_impr_name_std := str_db.strformat (v_arbc_impr_name);
         :importers.bus_id_exprd_dt := v_bus_id_exprd_dt;
         :importers.bus_id_issue_dt := v_bus_id_issue_dt;
         :importers.arbc_impr_name := v_arbc_impr_name;
         :importers.impr_name_std := v_impr_name_std;
         :importers.impr_address := v_impr_address;
         :importers.impr_tel_nbr := v_impr_tel_nbr;
         :importers.impr_fax_nbr := v_impr_fax_nbr;
         :importers.impr_telex_nbr := v_impr_telex_nbr;
         :importers.activity := v_activity;
         :importers.bus_id_status := v_bus_id_status;
         :importers.lst_tran_dt := SYSDATE;
         :importers.creation_flag := 3;

         IF p_type = 'B'
         THEN
            :importers.brnh_city_cd := v_city_code_of_id;

            BEGIN
               SELECT city_arb_name
                 INTO :importers.brnh_city_name
                 FROM cities
                WHERE city_cd = :importers.brnh_city_cd
                  AND ctry_cd = :ctry_cd_of_id;
            EXCEPTION
               WHEN OTHERS
               THEN
                  NULL;
            END;
         ELSIF p_type = 'M'
         THEN
            :importers.city_code_of_id := v_city_code_of_id;

            BEGIN
               SELECT city_arb_name
                 INTO :importers.id_city_name
                 FROM cities
                WHERE city_cd = :importers.city_code_of_id
                  AND ctry_cd = :ctry_cd_of_id;
            EXCEPTION
               WHEN OTHERS
               THEN
                  NULL;
            END;
         END IF;
      END IF;
   END IF;
END;
```

### PU EXEC_FUNS (Procedure, 119 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE EXEC_FUNS(CD VARCHAR2) IS

	PL_ID PARAMLIST;

  RET NUMBER;
pl_name      VARCHAR2(100) := 'TMPLT_MOD_CALL';
   P_Result NUMBER;
   P_Result_Msg VARCHAR2(100);
BEGIN

IF CD IN ('IMPR1110F4') THEN
  :PARAMETER.CODE :='IMPR1110F4';
END IF;

---------------------
IF CD IN ('IMPR1115_SCR') THEN
	pl_id := Get_Parameter_List(pl_name);
		IF NOT Id_Null(pl_id) THEN

	  ADD_PARAMETER(PL_ID,'P_IMPR_NO',TEXT_PARAMETER,:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR);

		END IF;
END IF;
----------------------


IF CD IN( 'IMPR1110F2','IMPR1110F1','IMPR1110F3','IMPR1110F5') THEN


  IF NOT ID_NULL(FIND_FORM('SUPPORT_ENTRY')) THEN
         CLOSE_FORM('SUPPORT_ENTRY');
  END IF;
  PL_ID := GET_PARAMETER_LIST('SUPPORT_ENTRY');

  IF ID_NULL(PL_ID) THEN
        PL_ID := CREATE_PARAMETER_LIST('SUPPORT_ENTRY');

        IF ID_NULL(PL_ID) THEN
           STR.DISPALERT('Error creating parameter list SUPPORT_ENTRY','SYSTEM_ERROR');
           RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
        END IF;

        ADD_PARAMETER(PL_ID,'WINDOW_X_POS',TEXT_PARAMETER,1.5);
	  ADD_PARAMETER(PL_ID,'WINDOW_Y_POS',TEXT_PARAMETER,2);
	  ADD_PARAMETER(PL_ID,'P_MODE',TEXT_PARAMETER,0);
	  ADD_PARAMETER(PL_ID,'P_CURR_PORT_CD',TEXT_PARAMETER,:IMPORTERS.PORT_CD);
	  CALL_FORM('SUPPORT_ENTRY.FMX',NO_HIDE,NO_REPLACE,NO_QUERY_ONLY,PL_ID);
	  DESTROY_PARAMETER_LIST(PL_ID);

 	 IF :GLOBAL.LETTER_NBR IS NOT NULL THEN
   	    :PARAMETER.CODE :=CD;
	    :PARAMETER.LETTER_NBR :=:GLOBAL.LETTER_NBR;

	    GO_BLOCK('IMPORTERS');
	   -- OPEN_FORM('SUPPORT_ENTRY.FMX');
       END IF;
   END IF;
END IF;


IF CD IN ('IMPR_CAR_AGENT') THEN
	:PARAMETER.CODE := 'IMPR1110SF6';
END IF;

IF CD IN ('IMPR_MCI_SERVICE') AND (:GLOBAL.ALLOW_MCI = 1 OR :GLOBAL.allow_moi
=1)THEN
	Update_Importer;
END IF;

IF CD IN ('CREATE_BRKR_IMPR_AUTH') THEN
	CREATE_BRKR_IMPR_AUTH;
END IF;


----------Abdelhadi02052018--------------
IF CD IN ('IMPR1115_GZT') THEN

BEGIN


Lib_Usr.Web_Services_Gazt.Pr_Import_Impr_Certificate(:IMPORTERS.Bus_Id, :IMPORTERS.Bus_Id_Brnch, P_Result, P_Result_Msg);


 IF P_Result = 3 THEN

 	  	str.dispalert('تم التحديث بنجاح','WARNING');
 ELSIF P_Result = '00' THEN

 	  	str.dispalert('رقم استدلالي غير صحيح','WARNING');
 ELSIF P_Result = '02' THEN

 	  	str.dispalert('رقم شهادة غير صحيح','WARNING');
 ELSIF P_Result = '04' THEN

 	  	str.dispalert('لا يوجد بيانات','WARNING');


 END IF;

    EXCEPTION
            WHEN OTHERS
            THEN
               NULL;
END;

END IF;
------------------------------
----------a.abdelhadi---update mobile_no -20/10/2020---------------
IF CD IN ('IMPR1110_MOB') THEN
	 IF  :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR > 3000000 THEN
     LOV_UPDATE_MOBLIE ;
	 ELSE
	 	STR.DISPALERT('رقم المستورد غير محدث ,, الرجاء مراجعة الدعم', 'ERROR');
	 	 RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
	 END IF;
END IF;
-------------------------------------------------------------------

END;
```

### PU CREATE_BRKR_IMPR_AUTH (Procedure, 60 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE CREATE_BRKR_IMPR_AUTH IS
BEGIN

IF (:PARAMETER.P_CLASS_CD = 1 AND :PARAMETER.P_USER_TYPE = 3)
	OR
   (:PARAMETER.P_USER_TYPE = 2 AND :PARAMETER.P_CLASS_CD = 23) THEN
DECLARE
V_ATHRZTN_SEQ_NBR NUMBER;
BEGIN

IF (:PARAMETER.P_CLASS_CD = 1 AND :PARAMETER.P_USER_TYPE = 3) THEN
V_ATHRZTN_SEQ_NBR := GNRL.GET_CUSTOMS_SEQ(:PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD,'BRKIMPS_AUTH_SEQ');
INSERT INTO BRKIMPS(
				PORT_CD,
				ATHRZTN_SEQ_NBR,
				IMPR_NBR,
				PORT_CD_BRKR,
				LIC_TYPE,
    				LIC_NBR,
    				ALLOW_EXPORT,
    				ATHRZTN_LETR_NBR,
    				ATHRZTN_LETR_DT,
    				ATHRZTN_TYPE_CD,
    				ATHRZTN_STRT_DT,
    				ATHRZTN_END_DT,
    				NOTE,
    				ATHRZTN_USG_NBR,
    				ATHRZTN_LAST_USG_DT,
    				ATHRZTN_CNCLD_DT)
			VALUES(:PORT_CD,
				V_ATHRZTN_SEQ_NBR,
				:IMPR_NBR,
				:PARAMETER.P_BRKR_PORT_CD,
				:PARAMETER.P_LIC_TYPE,
				:PARAMETER.P_BRKR_NBR,
				1,
				'تفويض الي ',
				HIJDATE_DB.CUR_HIJ_DATE,
				1,
				NULL,
				NULL,
				'تفويض بريد سريع',
				0,
				HIJDATE_DB.CUR_HIJ_DATE,
				NULL);

 IF NOT ACTIVITY.ADD_ACTIVITY(9, 2, 6, :IMPORTERS.Port_Cd||'!'||:IMPORTERS.Impr_Nbr) THEN
		RAISE FORm_Trigger_Failure;
 END IF;
end if;
STANDARD.COMMIT;
STR.DISPALERT('تم أنشاء التفويض','CFG_INFORMATION');
:GLOBAL.IMPR_NBR_B:=:IMPR_NBR;
:GLOBAL.IMPR_NAME_B:=:ARBC_IMPR_NAME;
:GLOBAL.BRKR_ATHRZTN_NBR_B:=V_ATHRZTN_SEQ_NBR;
END;
END IF;


END;
```

### PU CHK_PERSONAL (Procedure, 43 lines)

```plsql
-- CAN NOT BE CALLED BEFORE INSERT ONLY BEFORE UPDATE , BY (MAIH - 08/02/2015)
PROCEDURE chk_personal (
   P_impr_type_cd   NUMBER,
   P_hq_impr_nbr    NUMBER,
   P_STOP NUMBER DEFAULT 1)
IS
   v_new_type              NUMBER;
   v_old_type_personal     NUMBER;
   ex_personal_vs_others   EXCEPTION;
BEGIN
   v_new_type := P_impr_type_cd;

   SELECT COUNT (*)
     INTO v_old_type_personal
     FROM importers
    WHERE hq_impr_nbr = P_hq_impr_nbr AND impr_type_cd = 4
AND PORT_CD    = :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD;

   IF v_new_type = 4 AND v_old_type_personal = 0
   THEN
      RAISE ex_personal_vs_others;
   END IF;

   IF v_new_type <> 4 AND v_old_type_personal > 0
   THEN
      RAISE ex_personal_vs_others;
   END IF;
EXCEPTION
   WHEN ex_personal_vs_others
   THEN
	 :impr_type_cd  := :GLOBAL.OLD_impr_type_cd ;
	 SELECT IMPR_TYPE_NAME
	 INTO :IMPR_TYPE_NAME
	 FROM IMPR_TYPES
	 WHERE IMPR_TYPE_CD = :impr_type_cd ;


      STR.DISPALERT('لا يمكن تغيير نوع المستورد من شخصي الي نوع آخر أو العكس , يتم ذلك من خلال الدعم الفني التطبيقات فقط' ,'ERROR');

	IF P_STOP = 1 THEN
	      RAISE form_trigger_failure;
	END IF;
END;
```

### PU CHK_IMPR_DUPL (Procedure, 68 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE CHK_IMPR_DUPL IS
	V_COUNT NUMBER;
	V_IMPR_NAME VARCHAR2(200);
      V_IMPR_NBR  NUMBER;
BEGIN
	IF :BUS_ID_BRNCH IS NOT NULL THEN

		SELECT COUNT(*)
		INTO	V_COUNT
		FROM  IMPORTERS
		WHERE PORT_CD = :IMPORTERS.PORT_CD
		AND	CTRY_CD = NVL(:IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD, CTRY_CD)
		AND	BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
		AND 	BUS_ID  = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
		AND 	BUS_ID_BRNCH = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_BRNCH
		AND		((:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NULL) OR (:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NOT NULL AND IMPR_NBR <> :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR))
		;

		IF V_COUNT > 0 THEN
			SELECT ARBC_IMPR_NAME,IMPR_NBR
			INTO	V_IMPR_NAME,V_IMPR_NBR
			FROM IMPORTERS
			WHERE PORT_CD = :IMPORTERS.PORT_CD
			AND	CTRY_CD = NVL(:IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD, CTRY_CD)
			AND	BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
			AND 	BUS_ID  = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
			AND 	BUS_ID_BRNCH = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_BRNCH
			AND		((:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NULL) OR (:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NOT NULL AND IMPR_NBR <> :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR))
                  AND ROWNUM<=1
			;


			STR.DISPALERT(' لايمكن الإدخال، يوجد مستورد مدخل بنفس نوع المستورد ونفس الرقم الإستدلالي الرئيسي والفرعي ورقمه:  '||V_IMPR_NBR||' اسمه '||V_IMPR_NAME,'ERROR');
			RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
		END IF;
      END IF;
/*
	ELSE
		SELECT COUNT(*)
		INTO	V_COUNT
		FROM IMPORTERS
		WHERE PORT_CD = :IMPORTERS.PORT_CD
		AND	CTRY_CD = NVL(:IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD, CTRY_CD)
		AND	BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
		AND 	BUS_ID  =:IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
		AND 	BUS_ID_BRNCH IS NULL
		AND		((:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NULL) OR (:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NOT NULL AND IMPR_NBR <> :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR))
		;

		IF V_COUNT > 0 THEN
			SELECT MAX(IMPR_NBR)
			INTO	V_IMPR_NBR
			FROM IMPORTERS
			WHERE PORT_CD = :IMPORTERS.PORT_CD
			AND	CTRY_CD = NVL(:IMPORTERS.CTRY_CD, CTRY_CD)
			AND	BUS_ID_TYPE_CD = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD
			AND 	BUS_ID  = :IMPORTERS.BUS_ID
			AND 	BUS_ID_BRNCH IS NULL
			AND		((:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NULL) OR (:IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR IS NOT NULL AND IMPR_NBR <> :IMPORTERS.IMPR_NBR))
			;

			STR.DISPALERT(' لا يمكن تكرار الرقم الإستدلالي - الرقم الإستدلالي مستخدم لمستورد آخر مسلسله بالجمرك رقم : '||V_IMPR_NBR,'ERROR');
			RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
		END IF;

	END IF;
*/
END;
```

### PU CHK_DUPL_IMP_BRKR (Procedure, 29 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE CHK_DUPL_IMP_BRKR IS
	V_VAR_VALUE VARCHAR2(2) := 0;
	V_CHK       NUMBER;
	V_MSG		VARCHAR2(500);
BEGIN
	BEGIN
		SELECT VAR_VALUE
		INTO V_VAR_VALUE
		FROM SC_PORT_SETTING
		WHERE SYSOBJ_ID = 9
		AND VAR_NAME = 'CHK_DPLCATE_IMP_BRKR'
		AND PORT_CD    = :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD
		;
	EXCEPTION
	WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND  THEN
		V_VAR_VALUE := 0 ;
	END;

	IF V_VAR_VALUE = 1 THEN
  	  IF :IMPR_TYPE_CD IN (1,4) THEN
		V_CHK := IMPR.CHECK_DUPLICATE_IMP_BRKR(:PORT_CD,1,:IMPR_TYPE_CD,:BUS_ID,:COMM_RGSTRTN_TYPE,:BUS_ID,:BUS_ID_BRNCH,V_MSG);
		IF V_CHK = 1 THEN
			STR.DISPALERT(V_MSG,'ERROR');
			RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
		END IF;
	  END IF;
	END IF;

END;
```

### PU CHECK_IDNO (Function, 32 lines)

```plsql
FUNCTION CHECK_IDNO(ID_NO VARCHAR2) RETURN BOOLEAN IS

	RESULT 	NUMBER:=0;
	SU 		NUMBER:=0;
	CHR_RESULT 	VARCHAR2(10);

BEGIN

	FOR I IN 1..9 LOOP
		SU :=TO_NUMBER(SUBSTR(ID_NO,I,1));
			IF MOD(I,2)<>0 THEN
				RESULT:=RESULT + MOD(SU * 2,10);
				RESULT:=RESULT + TRUNC((SU * 2)/10);
			ELSE
				RESULT:=RESULT + SU;
			END IF;
	END LOOP;
	CHR_RESULT:=RESULT;

	IF SUBSTR(ID_NO,10,1)=0 THEN
	   IF SUBSTR(CHR_RESULT,LENGTH(CHR_RESULT),1)=0 THEN
	       RETURN TRUE;
	   END IF;
	ELSE
		IF TO_CHAR((10 - TO_NUMBER(SUBSTR(CHR_RESULT,LENGTH(CHR_RESULT),1))))=SUBSTR(ID_NO,10,1) THEN
       RETURN TRUE;
		END IF;

	END IF;

	RETURN FALSE;
END;
```

### PU CALL_LOV_COUNTRIES (Procedure, 43 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE CALL_LOV_COUNTRIES IS

   pl_id   ParamList;
   pl_name VARCHAR2(100) := 'BUS_ID_LOV';

   RET NUMBER;

 BEGIN
   pl_id := Get_Parameter_List(pl_name);
   IF Id_Null(pl_id) THEN
     pl_id := Create_Parameter_List(pl_name);
     IF Id_Null(pl_id) THEN
       ret := Str.Dispalert('Error creating parameter list '||pl_name, 'SYSTEM_ERROR');
       RAISE Form_Trigger_Failure;
     END IF;

   IF (:system.mode <> 'ENTER-QUERY'  ) THEN
	   Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'STATUS', TEXT_PARAMETER, 1);
   END IF;


  -- Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'WINDOW_WIDTH', TEXT_PARAMETER, 4.5);
   Add_Parameter(pl_id, 'WINDOW_TITLE', TEXT_PARAMETER, 'قائمة الدول');

   CALL_FORM('LOV_COUNTRIES.fmx',
              no_hide,
              no_replace,
              no_query_only,
              pl_id);

 	IF  :GLOBAL.CTRY_CD IS NOT NULL THEN
	:CTRY_CD := :GLOBAL.CTRY_CD;
	:CRTY_ARBC_NAME := :GLOBAL.CRTY_ARBC_NAME;
 	END IF;


   Destroy_Parameter_List(pl_id);
   ELSE
     ret := Str.DispAlert('Parameter list '||pl_name||' already exists!','INFORMATION');
     RAISE Form_Trigger_Failure;
   END IF;

END;
```

```plsql
PROCEDURE ADJ_FUNS IS
	V_COUNT 	NUMBER := 0;
	V_ROL_CNT NUMBER;
BEGIN


	IF   :PARAMETER.P_CLASS_CD=1 AND :PARAMETER.P_USER_TYPE=3 AND :IMPR_NBR IS NOT NULL THEN
		NULL;
	ELSE
		TMPLT.DISABLE_FUN('CREATE_BRKR_IMPR_AUTH');
	END IF;  -- HATEM 6-9-2015



--BILAL ALHABASHI
--01-02-2012
--FOR UPDATE IMPR TYPE IF USER FROM  ادارة القيود

	IF :PARAMETER.P_CLASS_CD = 19 AND :PARAMETER.P_USER_TYPE = 1 THEN
		SELECT COUNT(*) INTO V_ROL_CNT
		FROM   SC_USER_ROLES
		WHERE PORT_CD    = :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD
		and USER_ID = :PARAMETER.P_USER_ID
		AND    ROLE_ID = 'IMPR_UPD'
		;
		IF V_ROL_CNT > 0 AND :IMPR_TYPE_CD = 1  THEN
			TMPLT.ENABLE_FUN('IMPR1110F3');
		ELSE
			TMPLT.DISABLE_FUN('IMPR1110F3');
		END IF;
	END IF;

/*
TMPLT.DISABLE_FUN('IMPR_CAR_AGENT');

	SELECT COUNT(*)
	INTO   V_COUNT
	FROM   SC_USER_ROLES
	WHERE  PORT_CD    = :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD
	  AND  USER_ID    = :PARAMETER.P_USER_ID
	  AND  UR_ACTIVE  = 1
	  AND  ROLE_ID    = 'IMPR1110SF6';
 	IF NVL(V_COUNT,0) > 0 THEN
      	TMPLT.ENABLE_FUN('IMPR_CAR_AGENT');
	END IF;

*/
IF (NVL(:BUS_ID_TYPE_CD,0) = 2  AND :GLOBAL.ALLOW_MCI = 1 AND :IMPR_TYPE_CD <>12)OR (:IMPR_TYPE_CD = 4 AND :BUS_ID_TYPE_CD IN (3, 5) AND :GLOBAL.ALLOW_MOI = 1  )THEN
 TMPLT.ENABLE_FUN('IMPR_MCI_SERVICE');
ELSE
 TMPLT.DISABLE_FUN('IMPR_MCI_SERVICE');
END IF;
TMPLT.DISABLE_FUN('IMPR1110F2');

     IF :PARAMETER.P_CURR_PORT_CD <> 99 THEN
       TMPLT.DISABLE_FUN('IMPR1110_MOB');
     END IF;
EXCEPTION
	WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL;
END;
```

### PU VIEW_SAME_BUSID (Procedure, 47 lines)

```plsql
PROCEDURE VIEW_SAME_BUSID (V_PORT_CD NUMBER,V_BUS_ID VARCHAR2) IS

	CURSOR C_IMP	IS
	SELECT IMPR_NBR,IMPR_TYPE_CD,BUS_ID_TYPE_CD,BUS_ID
	FROM 	IMPORTERS
	WHERE PORT_CD= V_PORT_CD
	AND   BUS_ID = V_BUS_ID ;

	V_ITEMS	VARCHAR2(5000):=NULL;
	V_DESC	VARCHAR2(300);
	V_DESC1	VARCHAR2(300);
	V_RET		VARCHAR2(100) ;
	V_IMP_TYP_NAME VARCHAR2(40);
	V_BUS_TYP_NAME VARCHAR2(40);

BEGIN
	V_DESC :='الرقم الإستدلالي المدخل مستخدم في قائمة المستوردين التالية : ' || CHR(10) || CHR(13)|| CHR(10) || CHR(13);
	FOR I IN C_IMP LOOP
			SELECT 	IMPR_TYPE_NAME
			INTO		V_IMP_TYP_NAME
			FROM		IMPR_TYPES
			WHERE		IMPR_TYPE_CD = I.IMPR_TYPE_CD ;

			SELECT	BUS_ID_TYPE_NAME
			INTO 		V_BUS_TYP_NAME
			FROM 		BUS_ID_TYPES
			WHERE 	BUS_ID_TYPE_CD =I.BUS_ID_TYPE_CD;

			V_ITEMS :=V_ITEMS ||'     رقم المستورد: '|| I.IMPR_NBR || '  -  ' || V_BUS_TYP_NAME ||'/ '||I.BUS_ID|| CHR(10) || CHR(13);
	END LOOP;

	V_DESC1 := CHR(10) || CHR(13) || 'هل تريد استخدام نفس الرقم الإستدلالي ؟؟؟؟' ;

      IF V_ITEMS IS NOT NULL THEN
		V_ITEMS := V_DESC  	|| V_ITEMS  || V_DESC1 ;
		V_ITEMS := CHR(1000)    || V_ITEMS ;
		V_RET := INTRFACE.FLASH_ALERT(V_ITEMS, 'CONFIRM', 2, ' تنبيه ',
                              5.5, 1, 1.2, 0.5, 1300, 'r255g0b0', 6);
		IF V_RET = ALERT_BUTTON2 THEN
			:GLOBAL.CHK_DUP_BUSID := 0 ;
			:BUS_ID := NULL;
           		RAISE FORM_TRIGGER_FAILURE;
		ELSE
			:GLOBAL.CHK_DUP_BUSID := 1 ;
		END IF;
	END IF;
END;
```
